- It uses SLSQP with alpha up to 200,000,000. Objective: m + (softmax(alpha*(g-m)))/alpha. For large alpha, numerical instability. SLSQP ftol 1e-18. It may spend huge time per alpha. Use loops over positive/negative with slices:
```
def grad_lse(h, q):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # q index idx = k + n -1
    # k positive 1..n-1
    for k in range(1,n):
       qv=q[n-1+k]
       if abs(qv)<1e-15: continue
       cnt=n-k
       g[k: k+cnt] += qv*(1-h[:cnt])   # term A i=j+k
       g[:cnt] -= qv*h[:cnt]              # term B i=j
    # k negative -r
    for r in range(1,n):
       qv=q[n-1-r]
       if abs(qv)<1e-15: continue
       cnt=n-r
       # k=-r: term A i=j-r, j=r..n-1, i=j-r. g[:cnt] += qv*(1-h[r:])?
       # term B i=j+r, j=0..n-1-r: g[r:] -= qv*h[:cnt]
       g[:cnt] += qv*(1-h[r:])
       g[r:] -= qv*h[:cnt]
    # k=0: c_0 = sum h_i(1-h_i). derivative = 1-2h?
    q0=q[n-1]
    g += q0*(1-2h)
    return g
```

def corr_vec(h): return np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
def c5_from_corr(c,n): return float(np.max(c)*2.0/n)

Feasible project with exact sum:
```
def project(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2.0
   for _ in range(100):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
      if diff>0: room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; h+=diff/s*room
      else: s=h.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; h += diff/s*h
   # final exact? If not within 1e-10, adjust one near nonboundary.
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      idx=np.argsort(np.minimum(h,1-h)); # choose variable with room? Want change by diff within box.
      # choose variable where possible: if diff>0 and h[idx]<1, add; if diff<0 h>0.
      add=diff
      for idx in ...:
         if diff>0 and h[idx]<1:
             add=min(add,1-h[idx])? better spread.
   return h
```
Could implement exact balancing by selecting variable with enough margin and adjust; but if diff large maybe loop. Use平分? Since previous project likely enough. We can enforce close to 1e-12.

Define top-level:
```
def eval_lse(x, beta, n):
   c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*(2.0/n)
   m=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-m)
   z-=z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return m
   return m + np.log(s)/beta
```
Wait if z normalized, lse = m + (max z + log(sum exp(z-max)))/beta? Using z=β(c-m); lse(β(c-m)) = maxz + log(Σe^{z-maxz}). Objective = m + that/beta. yes.
```
def eval_lse_grad(x,beta,n):
   c=...; grad_lse_c(x,q)*dx
```

We can update all rows vectorized:
```
def update_D_after_shift(D,n,a,da,b,db):
   # for each a with delta da
   idx = np.arange(n)
   k1 = idx - a  # p-a
   mask = (k1>=-(n-1))&(k1<=n-1)
   D[mask, n-1 + k1[mask]] -= da
   k2 = a - idx
   mask2 = (k2>=-(n-1))&(k2<=n-1)
   D[mask2, n-1 + k2[mask2]] -= da
```

Let's write a strong binary hill-climbing:
- Start from several binary seeds:
   a) threshold of parent.
   b) random balanced sequences with block structure: e.g. half ones sorted with random shifts? We can generate via `bits = rng.randint` and choose top half; then order? Random bits uniform.
   c) periodic guessed: e.g. use 3-4 repeating patterns optimized? But local search can improve.
- For each seed, perform first-improvement swaps with a time limit. To avoid O(n^2) all pairs, use simulated annealing: random zero/one, compute delta and new max, accept if new max < current (or equal with small prob). Keep best. Since n small, 200k moves per seed.
- We can use target threshold Mcurrent: if c has max M, candidate accepted if newmax < M, or newmax == M and (some secondary objective like sum of top10) improves. This helps plateaus.
- Since evaluating newmax via np.max(c+d) O(L). Could maintain active top: if newmax < M, accept; else compute maybe quickly? `np.max` is C fast.
- Use random restarts after no improvement.

Implement `random_local_search(h,n,max_moves,deadline,init_temp?)`:
Pseudo:
```
h = to_binary(h,n).astype(float)
c = corr(h)
M = c.max()
best_c = M; best_h=h.copy()
zeros = list(np.where(h==0)[0]); ones=list(np.where(h==1)[0])
trials=0
stuck=0
while time<deadline and trials<max_moves:
   i = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
   j = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
   d = delta(...); newc = c + d; tm = newc.max()
   if tm < M or (tm == M and secondary lower):
       h[i],h[j]=1,0; update lists; c += d; M=tm; best update; stuck=0
   else:
       # simulated annealing: if tm < M+delta_accept? maybe accept rarely to escape
       if rng.rand() < exp(-(tm-M)/? maybe 0.001): accept temporarily? But for binary, accepting worse can find. Need maintain best.
```

Gradient `grad_counts(h,q)`:
```
def _grad_counts(h,q):
    n=h.size; L=2*n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
    center=n-1
    q0=q[center]
    if q0 !=0: g += q0*(1-2*h)
    omh = 1-h
    for r in range(1,n):
       qv=q[center+r]
       if qv:
          cnt=n-r
          g[r:r+cnt] += qv*omh[:cnt]
          g[:cnt] -= qv*h[:cnt]
          qv=q[center-r]
          if qv:
             g[:cnt] += qv*omh[r:]
             g[r:] -= qv*h[:cnt]
    return g
```

  g=np.zeros(n)

Let's design `run_slsqp_once(h,n,beta,maxiter,deadline)`:
```
def run_slsqp_once(h,n,beta,maxiter):
   x0=project(h,n)
   try:
      res=minimize(_lse_fun,x0,jac=_lse_jac,args=(beta,n),method='SLSQP',bounds=BOUNDS[n]? constraints=CONS, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      x=project(np.clip(res.x,0,1),n)
   except: return h
   return x
```

```

`project_feasible`:
Use iterative proportional fit; final exact adjust. Need ensure sum exactly n/2 to machine. Implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        d=target-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-12: break
        if d>0:
            r=1.0-h; s=r.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h += d/s * r
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h += d/s * h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    d=target-h.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-11:
       # distribute to variable with largest margin; loop possibly
       # choose order by margin if d>0: 1-h, else h
       if d>0:
          order=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
          rem=d
          for idx in order:
             add=min(rem,1-h[idx])
             h[idx]+=add; rem-=add
             if rem<=1e-14: break
       else:
          order=np.argsort(h) # smallest? want reduce where possible; largest h first? use -h
          rem=-d
          for idx in np.argsort(h)[::-1]:
             sub=min(rem,h[idx])
             h[idx]-=sub; rem-=sub
             if rem<=1e-14: break
    return h
```
If clip after distribution may alter? We clip by min. Good. But if after final distribution not exact due floating, loop maybe. Could be enough.

`run_seed`:
```
def run_seed(n,rng,mean_run):
    bits=[]; val=rng.randint(2)
    while len(bits)<n:
       ln=int(rng.geometric(min(mean_run,10)))? geometric p=1/mean_run gives mean; cap 1..20.
       bits.extend([val]*ln)
       val=1-val
    bits=np.array(bits[:n],float)
    return to_binary(bits,n)
```
Need algorithmic, no literal large. Use `rng.geometric(1.0/mean_run)` with stop? If p too small, mean. Cap maybe `min(1+int(rng.exponential(mean_run-1)), 30)`. Simpler: `ln = rng.randint(1, max(2, int(mean_run*2)))`. This yields uniform lengths. Could create blocky seed. Use `mean_run` like 3,5,8.

`interp_rescale`:
```
def interp_rescale(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old==n_new: return project_feasible(h,n_new)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False) + (2.0/n_old)/2
    xn = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False) + (2.0/n_new)/2
    hh=np.interp(xn,xo,h)
    return project_feasible(hh,n_new)
```
Could use endpoint? Centers okay.

`_lse_fun`: We need stable logsumexp. Since beta can be large, scale c to about [0,1]. Use:
```
c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*(2.0/n)
m=c.max()
z=beta*(c-m)
zmax=z.max()
s=np.exp(z-zmax).sum()
return m + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta
```
If beta small, zmax maybe 0 after subtract? Wait z=β(c-m), max should be 0 (within) but due floating maybe. If we subtract zmax, formula: m + (zmax? originally β(c_i-m), max shifted?). Let `y=β*(c-m)`. lse(y)=ymax+log(sum exp(y-ymax)). Objective=m + lse(y)/β. Our z=β*(c-m); `zmax=z.max()` (should 0). return m + (zmax + log(sum exp(z-zmax)))/β. Good.
Could just do `z=beta*c; zmax=z.max(); return (zmax+log(sum exp(z-zmax)))/beta`. This avoids m and possible β*(c-m) underflow less. Use full lse:
```
z=beta*c; zmax=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-zmax); return (zmax+np.log(e.sum()))/beta
```
This equals lse(β c)/β = m + ... Good. Use this.
For objective maybe around 0.4. If beta huge, zmax huge; subtract stable.

`_lse_jac`: compute c scaled and q = softmax(β c) (same stable without beta? q=e^{βc}/Σ). `z=β*c; z-=z.max(); e=np.exp(z); q=e/e.sum()`. Then grad_counts* (2/n).
Potential: For beta huge, z after subtract max may underflow many to0, q concentrated. Good.

`slsqp_once`:
```
def slsqp_once(h,n,beta,maxiter,ftol=1e-12):
    x=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n)]? Actually `[(0.0,1.0)]*n`
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_cons_fun,'jac':_cons_jac}]
    try:
      res=minimize(_lse_fun,x,args=(beta,n),jac=_lse_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol,'disp':0})
      x=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
    except Exception:
      return x0
    return project_feasible(x,n)
```
Creating bounds each call; okay. Need import minimize.

def polish_slsqp(h,n,betas,deadline, max_iter_factor=1.0):
    base=max(50, min(700, int(120000.0/n)))
    for beta in betas:
       if time.time()>deadline-5: break
       mi=int(base*max_iter_factor*(1.0 if beta<1000 else 0.6))
       h=slsqp_once(h,n,beta,mi)
    return h

`local_search_binary`: Need code not too long. Let's write robust.
```
def local_search_binary(h,n,deadline,rng):
    if n>1000: # fallback small moves? maybe skip
       return to_binary(h,n), compute_c5(...)
    hb = to_binary(h,n)
    c = np.correlate(hb, 1-hb, 'full').astype(float)
    center=n-1; L=2*n-1; M=int(np.max(c))
    best_h=hb.copy(); bestM=M
    zeros = [i for i in range(n) if hb[i]==0]
    ones = [i for i in range(n) if hb[i]==1]
    ar=np.arange(n)
    idx1 = (center + ar[:,None] - ar[None,:]).astype(np.intp)
    idx2 = (center + ar[None,:] - ar[:,None]).astype(np.intp)
    d = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    temp = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - hb
    moves=0; stuck=0; last_improve=time.time()
    while time.time()<deadline:
       # decide mode 1/3 random, 1/3 directed direct pair, 1/3 random active pair? Use loop counter.
       mode = moves % 6
       # choose i,j
       chosen = None
       if mode == 3 or mode == 4:
          # active direct pair
          act = np.where(c >= M-1)[0]  # recompute each time? expensive but active every few. Could maintain.
          if act.size:
             l = act[rng.randint(act.size)]
             k = l-center
             if k>0:
                p = rng.randint(n-k)
                while hb[p]==1 or hb[p+k]==0:
                    p=rng.randint(n-k)
                    # limit? maybe 10 tries
                i=p; j=p+k
             elif k<0:
                r=-k; p=rng.randint(n-r)
                while hb[p]==0 or hb[p+r]==1:
                    p=rng.randint(n-r)
                i=p; j=p+r
             else: continue
       elif mode == 5:
          # one from active pair and random zero
          ... maybe skip
       if chosen None:
          if not zeros or not ones: break
          i=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; j=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
       # If i==j skip
       d.fill(0.0)
       d[idx1[i]] += omh
       d[idx2[i]] -= hb
       d[idx1[j]] -= omh
       d[idx2[j]] += hb
       d[center] -= 2.0
       d[center + i - j] += 1.0
       d[center + j - i] += 1.0
       np.add(c,d,out=temp)
       tm = int(temp.max())  # c integer but float
       accept = False
       if tm < M:
          accept=True
       elif tm == M and (rng.rand() < 0.03):
          accept=True
       elif tm == M+1 and (rng.rand() < 0.002):
          accept=True
       if accept:
          # update h,omh,c, lists
          hb[i]=1.0; hb[j]=0.0; omh[i]=0.0; omh[j]=1.0; c += d; M=tm
          # lists remove
          try: zeros.remove(i); ones.remove(j)
          except ValueError: pass # should not
          zeros.append(j); ones.append(i)
          if M < bestM:
             bestM=M; best_h=hb.copy(); last_improve=time.time(); stuck=0
          moves += 1
       else:
          moves += 1
          if tm >= M: 
             # maybe no improvement; stuck++?
          if time.time() - last_improve > 30 and stuck>??? : restart? Could just random reset to best_h with few swaps.
```

Implement stable sigmoid:
```
def _sigmoid(z):
   out=np.empty_like(z)
   pos=z>=0
   out[pos]=1/(1+np.exp(-z[pos]))
   ez=np.exp(z[~pos]); out[~pos]=ez/(1+ez)
   return out
```
For z within ±20, simple `1/(1+np.exp(-z))` safe? exp(20)=4.8e8, ok; exp(-20)=2e-9. No overflow. Use simple with clip: `z=np.clip(z,-50,50)`. Good.

L-BFGS-B objective and jac:
```
def _lse_p_obj(p,beta,n):
   h=_h_from_p(p,n)
   return _obj_lse_h(h,beta,n)
def _obj_lse_h(h,beta,n):
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')*(2/n)
   z=beta*c; zmax=z.max(); return (zmax+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zmax))))/beta
```
For beta large, zmax large; stable.
Jac:
```
def _lse_p_jac(p,beta,n):
   h=_h_from_p(p,n)
   c=...
   q=_softmax_q(c,beta)
   G=_grad_counts(h,q)*(2/n)
   s=h*(1-h)
   sd=s.sum()
   if sd<1e-14: return np.zeros(n-1)
   H=np.dot(s,G)
   grad = s[:-1]*(G[:-1] - H/sd)
   return grad
```
Does this gradient correctly account for p_last=0? Yes. The parameterization maps p to t with last fixed 0. b is sum constraint. We derived with t_i changes for i last: for p_i (i<n-1), δt_i=1, δt_last=0; δb=-s_i/Sd. Then δh_j=s_j(δ_{ij}+δb). Good.

Newton/bisection details: Bisection monotonic in b. If p all -30, b≈30. Range ±100 enough. Implementation:
```
def _h_from_p(p,n):
    t=np.empty(n); t[:n-1]=p; t[n-1]=0.0
    lo=-100.0; hi=100.0
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=1/(1+exp(-clip(t+mid)))
       if s.sum() < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    b=0.5*(lo+hi)
    s=1/(1+exp(-clip(t+b)))
    return s
```

Now, possible bug: `_h_from_p` bisection using target half but p has n-1; if n large and p bounds, sum monotonic. Bisection 60? Let's implement 45 plus Newton.
```
def _h_from_p(p,n):
    t=np.empty(n,float)
    t[:-1]=p; t[n-1]=0.0
    target=0.5*n
    lo=-100.0; hi=100.0
    for _ in range(45):
        b=0.5*(lo+hi)
        s=_sigmoid(t+b)
        if s.sum() < target: lo=b
        else: hi=b
    b=0.5*(lo+hi)
    for _ in range(6):
        s=_sigmoid(t+b)
        f=s.sum()-target
        if abs(f)<1e-12: break
        ds=s*(1.0-s).sum()
        if ds<=1e-15: break
        b -= f/ds
    return _sigmoid(t+b)
```
Newton may overshoot outside [-100,100]? f small, fine. Could clip b to [-100,100].
Sigmoid stable:
```
def _sigmoid(z):
    return 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-np.clip(z,-50,50)))
```

`h_to_p`:
```
def h_to_p(h,n):
    eps=1e-12
    hh=np.clip(h,eps,1-eps)
    Lh=np.log(hh)-np.log1p(-hh)
    return np.clip(Lh[:-1]-Lh[-1], -40,40)
```

Now, local_search_binary may be slow if it cycles equal accept. Use move counter and best. Need ensure it returns before deadline. It recreates idx arrays each call, can be memory. Good.

`local_search_binary` top-level code:
```
def local_search_binary(h,n,deadline,rng):
    if n<4 or n%2: return to_binary(h,n), compute_c5(...)
    hb=to_binary(h,n).astype(np.int8)
    oh=1-hb
    c=np.correlate(hb,oh,'full').astype(np.int32)
    M=int(c.max())
    best_h=hb.copy(); bestM=M; last=time.time()
    if M==0: return best_h, 2.0*bestM/n
    center=n-1; L=2*n-1
    ar=np.arange(n,dtype=np.intp)
    idx1=(center + ar[:,None] - ar[None,:]).astype(np.intp)
    idx2=(center + ar[None,:] - ar[:,None]).astype(np.intp)
    d=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int8)
    temp=np.empty(L, dtype=np.int32)
    moves=0
    while time.time()<deadline and M>0:
       i=None
       if moves & 1:
          act=np.flatnonzero(c >= M-1)
          if act.size:
             l=int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
             k=l-center
             i=j=None
             if k>0:
                cnt=n-k
                for _ in range(30):
                   p=rng.randint(cnt)
                   if hb[p]==0 and hb[p+k]==1: i=p; j=p+k; break
                if i is None:
                   pairs=np.flatnonzero((hb[:cnt]==0)&(hb[k:k+cnt]==1))
                   if pairs.size: p=int(pairs[rng.randint(pairs.size)]); i=p; j=p+k
             elif k<0:
                r=-k; cnt=n-r
                for _ in range(30):
                   p=rng.randint(cnt)
                   if hb[p]==1 and hb[p+r]==0: i=p; j=p+r; break
                if i is None:
                   pairs=np.flatnonzero((hb[:cnt]==1)&(hb[r:r+cnt]==0))
                   if pairs.size: p=int(pairs[rng.randint(pairs.size)]); i=p; j=p+r
       if i is None:
          for _ in range(6):
             ii=rng.randint(n); jj=rng.randint(n)
             if ii==jj: continue
             if hb[ii]==0 and hb[jj]==1: i=ii; j=jj; break
             if hb[ii]==1 and hb[jj]==0: i=jj; j=ii; break
       if i is None:
          continue
       # if i==j or values changed? ensure
       if hb[i]==hb[j]: continue
       if hb[i]==1: i,j=j,i
       # if still not zero/one skip
       d.fill(0)
       d[idx1[i]] += oh
       d[idx2[i]] -= hb
       d[idx1[j]] -= oh
       d[idx2[j]] += hb
       d[center] -= 2
       d[center+i-j] += 1
       d[center+j-i] += 1
       np.add(c,d,out=temp)
       tm=int(temp.max())
       accept = tm < M
       if not accept and tm == M and rng.rand()<0.08: accept=True
       if not accept and tm == M+1 and rng.rand()<0.003: accept=True
       if accept:
          hb[i]=1; hb[j]=0; oh[i]=0; oh[j]=1; c += d; M=tm
          if M < bestM:
             bestM=M; best_h=hb.copy(); last=time.time()
          if M > bestM + 3:
             hb=best_h.copy(); oh=1-hb; c=np.correlate(hb,oh,'full').astype(np.int32); M=int(c.max())
          if time.time()-last>25.0:
             # perturb best
             if bestM>0:
                for _ in range(10):
                   ii=rng.randint(n); jj=rng.randint(n)
                   if ii!=jj and hb[ii]==0 and hb[jj]==1: hb[ii],hb[jj]=1,0; oh[ii],oh[jj]=0,1
                   elif ii!=jj and hb[ii]==1 and hb[jj]==0: hb[ii],hb[jj]=0,1; oh[ii],oh[jj]=1,0
                c=np.correlate(hb,oh,'full').astype(np.int32); M=int(c.max())
             last=time.time()
       moves += 1
    return best_h.astype(float), compute_c5(best_h,n)
```

Term A_i = ∑_j a_j q_{i-j}, where j=0..n-1, and k=i-j. This is convolution of a and a sequence Q where Q_k = q_k for integer k, zero otherwise. If we create array `Q` length 2n-1 with index k+center? So A = np.convolve(a, Qk, 'full')[i+center] for i=0..n-1. Good.
Term B_i = ∑_j b_j q_{j-i}? Because -∑_k q_k h_{i+k}; set j=i+k -> k=j-i, sum over j where k in range. B_i = ∑_j b_j q_{j-i} = ∑_j b_j q_{-(i-j)}. Let's update `_grad_counts`:
```
def _grad_counts(h,q):
    n=h.size; center=n-1
    a=1.0-h
    return np.convolve(a,q,'full')[center:center+n] - np.convolve(h,q[::-1],'full')[center:center+n]
```

`project_feasible`: Keep concise but exact. Use iterative fit; final distribution. Need if `n` int.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h += diff/s*room
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-15: break
            h += diff/s*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1.0-h)[::-1]
       else:
          order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       rem=diff
       for idx in order:
          if rem>0:
             add=min(rem,1.0-h[idx])
             if add>0: h[idx]+=add; rem-=add
          else:
             sub=min(-rem,h[idx])
             if sub>0: h[idx]-=sub; rem+=sub
          if abs(rem)<1e-14: break
    return h
```
If iterative fit leaves tiny, final ok. Could final adjust by adding small to variable with largest margin. Good.

`to_binary`:
```
def to_binary(h,n):
    hb=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n>0:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]
       hb[idx]=1.0
    return hb
```

```
def run_seed(n,rng,mean_run=5):
    bits=[]; val=int(rng.randint(2)); max_run=max(2,int(round(mean_run*2)))
    while len(bits)<n:
       ln=int(rng.randint(1, max_run+1))
       ln=min(ln, n-len(bits))
       bits.extend([val]*ln)
       val=1-val
    return to_binary(np.array(bits[:n], float), n)
```

def _lse_p_jac(p,beta,n):
    h=_h_from_p(p,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')*(2.0/n)
    q=_softmax_q(c,beta)
    dx=2.0/n
    G=dx*_grad_counts(h,q)
    s=h*(1.0-h)
    sd=s.sum()
    if sd<1e-14: return np.zeros(n-1)
    H=float(np.dot(s,G))
    return s[:-1]*(G[:-1]-H/sd)
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
            rem = diff
            for idx in order:
                add = min(rem, 1.0 - h[idx])
                if add > 0.0:
                    h[idx] += add
                    rem -= add
                if abs(rem) < 1e-14:
                    break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
            rem = -diff
            for idx in order:
                sub = min(rem, h[idx])
                if sub > 0.0:
                    h[idx] -= sub
                    rem -= sub
                if abs(rem) < 1e-14:
                    break
    return h


def to_binary(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n // 2]
        hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb


def random_binary(n, rng):
    x = rng.rand(n)
    return to_binary(x, n)


def run_seed(n, rng, mean_run=5):
    bits = []
    val = int(rng.randint(0, 2))
    max_run = max(2, int(round(2.0 * mean_run)))
    while len(bits) < n:
        ln = int(rng.randint(1, max_run + 1))
        ln = min(ln, n - len(bits))
        bits.extend([val] * ln)
        val = 1 - val
    return to_binary(np.array(bits[:n], dtype=float), n)


def interp_rescale(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    xo = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    xn = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n_new)


def _sigmoid(z):
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)))


def _h_from_p(p, n):
    t = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    t[:n - 1] = p
    t[n - 1] = 0.0
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    lo, hi = -100.0, 100.0
    for _ in range(45):
        b = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = _sigmoid(t + b)
        if s.sum() < target:
            lo = b
        else:
            hi = b
    b = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    for _ in range(6):
        s = _sigmoid(t + b)
        f = s.sum() - target
        if abs(f) < 1e-12:
            break
        ds = s.sum() if False else float((s * (1.0 - s)).sum())
        if ds < 1e-15:
            break
        b = max(-100.0, min(100.0, b - f / ds))
    return _sigmoid(t + b)


def h_to_p(h, n):
    eps = 1e-12
    hh = np.clip(h, eps, 1.0 - eps)
    Lh = np.log(hh) - np.log1p(-hh)
    return np.clip(Lh[:-1] - Lh[-1], -40.0, 40.0)


def _obj_lse_h(h, beta, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    return (zmax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zmax)))) / beta


def _softmax_q(c, beta):
    z = beta * c
    z = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.size)
    return e / s


def _grad_counts(h, q):
    n = h.size
    center = n - 1
    slash_start = center
    slash_end = center + n
    a = 1.0 - h
    return (
        np.convolve(a, q, mode="full")[slash_start:slash_end]
        - np.convolve(h, q[::-1], mode="full")[slash_start:slash_end]
    )


def _lse_p_obj(p, beta, n):
    h = _h_from_p(p, n)
    return _obj_lse_h(h, beta, n)


def _lse_p_jac(p, beta, n):
    h = _h_from_p(p, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))
    q = _softmax_q(c, beta)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = dx * _grad_counts(h, q)
    s = h * (1.0 - h)
    sd = float(s.sum())
    if sd < 1e-14:
        return np.zeros(n - 1)
    H = float(np.dot(s, g))
    return s[:-1] * (g[:-1] - H / sd)


def polish_lse(h, n, betas, deadline, factor=1.0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    p = h_to_p(h, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (n - 1)
    base = max(60, min(450, int(180000.0 / max(1, n))))
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2:
            break
        mi = int(base * factor * (0.72 if beta > 5000.0 else 1.0))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lse_p_obj,
                p,
                args=(beta, n),
                jac=_lse_p_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": mi, "ftol": 1e-12, "maxls": 30, "maxfun": max(100, mi * 30)},
            )
            p = res.x
        except Exception:
            p = best_p
            continue
        h_new = project_feasible(_h_from_p(p, n), n)
        c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new.copy()
            best_p = p.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def local_search_binary(h, n, deadline, rng):
    if n < 4 or (n % 2) or n > 1200:
        hb = to_binary(h, n)
        return hb, compute_c5(hb, n)

    hb = to_binary(h, n).astype(np.int8)
    oh = (1 - hb).astype(np.int8)
    c = np.correlate(hb, oh, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
    M = int(c.max())
    best_h = hb.copy()
    bestM = M
    last = time.time()
    if M <= 0:
        return best_h.astype(float), 0.0

    center = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    ar = np.arange(n, dtype=np.intp)
    idx1 = center + ar[:, None] - ar[None, :]
    idx2 = center + ar[None, :] - ar[:, None]
    d = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int8)
    temp = np.empty(L, dtype=np.int32)
    moves = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and M > 0:
        i = None
        j = None

        if moves & 1:
            act = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - 1)
            if act.size:
                ell = int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
                k = ell - center
                if k > 0:
                    cnt = n - k
                    for _ in range(25):
                        pp = int(rng.randint(cnt))
                        if hb[pp] == 0 and hb[pp + k] == 1:
                            i, j = pp, pp + k
                            break
                    if i is None:
                        pairs = np.flatnonzero((hb[:cnt] == 0) & (hb[k:k + cnt] == 1))
                        if pairs.size:
                            pp = int(pairs[rng.randint(pairs.size)])
                            i, j = pp, pp + k
                elif k < 0:
                    r = -k
                    cnt = n - r
                    for _ in range(25):
                        pp = int(rng.randint(cnt))
                        if hb[pp] == 1 and hb[pp + r] == 0:
                            i, j = pp, pp + r
                            break
                    if i is None:
                        pairs = np.flatnonzero((hb[:cnt] == 1) & (hb[r:r + cnt] == 0))
                        if pairs.size:
                            pp = int(pairs[rng.randint(pairs.size)])
                            i, j = pp, pp + r

        if i is None:
            for _ in range(8):
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                if hb[ii] == 0 and hb[jj] == 1:
                    i, j = ii, jj
                    break
                if hb[ii] == 1 and hb[jj] == 0:
                    i, j = jj, ii
                    break

        if i is None or i == j or hb[i] == hb[j]:
            moves += 1
            continue
        if hb[i] == 1:
            i, j = j, i

        d.fill(0)
        d[idx1[i]] += oh
        d[idx2[i]] -= hb
        d[idx1[j]] -= oh
        d[idx2[j]] += hb
        d[center] -= 2
        d[center + i - j] += 1
        d[center + j - i] += 1

        np.add(c, d, out=temp)
        tm = int(temp.max())

        accept = tm < M
        if not accept and tm == M and rng.rand() < 0.08:
            accept = True
        if not accept and tm == M + 1 and rng.rand() < 0.003:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            hb[i] = 1
            hb[j] = 0
            oh[i] = 0
            oh[j] = 1
            c += d
            M = tm
            if M < bestM:
                bestM = M
                best_h = hb.copy()
                last = time.time()

            if M > bestM + 3 or time.time() - last > 25.0:
                hb = best_h.copy().astype(np.int8)
                oh = (1 - hb).astype(np.int8)
                c = np.correlate(hb, oh, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
                M = int(c.max())
                for _ in range(12):
                    ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                    jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if ii == jj:
                        continue
                    if hb[ii] == 0 and hb[jj] == 1:
                        hb[ii], hb[jj] = 1, 0
                        oh[ii], oh[jj] = 0, 1
                    elif hb[ii] == 1 and hb[jj] == 0:
                        hb[ii], hb[jj] = 0, 1
                        oh[ii], oh[jj] = 1, 0
                c = np.correlate(hb, oh, mode="full").astype(np.int32)
                M = int(c.max())
                last = time.time()

        moves += 1

    return best_h.astype(float), float(bestM) * (2.0 / float(n))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 10.0
    dl = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 400

    if n0 < 64 or h0 is None:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)
    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        n0 = max(2, n0 - 1)
        h0 = interp_rescale(h0, n0 + 1, n0)

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_n = int(n0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    p1 = t0 + 0.08 * total
    h1, c1 = polish_lse(best_h, best_n, (10, 50, 200, 1000), p1)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_c, best_h, best_n = c1, h1, best_n

    p2 = t0 + 0.28 * total
    if time.time() < p2 - 4 and best_n % 2 == 0:
        hb, cb = local_search_binary(best_h, best_n, min(p2 - 4, time.time() + 0.10 * total), rng)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c, best_h, best_n = cb, hb, best_n
        if time.time() < p2 - 3:
            h2, c2 = polish_lse(best_h, best_n, (100, 500, 2000, 10000), p2 - 3)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = c2, h2
    if time.time() < p2 - 2 and best_n % 2 == 0:
        seed_h = run_seed(best_n, rng, 8)
        hb, cb = local_search_binary(seed_h, best_n, p2 - 2, rng)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c, best_h, best_n = cb, hb, best_n

    p3 = t0 + 0.68 * total
    base_res = (80, 120, 160, 200, 240, 320, 400, 480, 560)
    res = []
    for nh in list(base_res) + [best_n]:
        nh = int(nh)
        if nh < 60 or nh > 800 or (nh % 2):
            continue
        if nh not in res:
            res.append(nh)
    res.sort()

    weights = [max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(nh))) for nh in res]
    rem_w = float(sum(weights))
    for w, nh in zip(weights, res):
        if len(res) <= 1 or time.time() >= p3 - 8:
            break
        t_end = min(p3, time.time() + max(4.0, (p3 - time.time()) * (w / max(1e-12, rem_w))))
        rem_w -= w
        if t_end <= time.time():
            continue
        h_init = interp_rescale(best_h, best_n, nh)
        hh, ch = polish_lse(h_init, nh, (10, 100, 500, 2000), t_end)
        if ch < best_c:
            best_c, best_h, best_n = ch, hh, int(nh)
        if nh <= 320 and time.time() < p3 - 5:
            h_rand = run_seed(nh, rng, 6)
            hh, ch = polish_lse(h_rand, nh, (10, 100, 500), min(p3 - 2, time.time() + 0.5 * max(1.0, t_end - time.time())))
            if ch < best_c:
                best_c, best_h, best_n = ch, hh, int(nh)

    h_final, c_final = polish_lse(best_h, best_n, (2000, 10000, 50000, 200000), dl - 8)
    if c_final < best_c:
        best_c, best_h = c_final, h_final

    for _ in range(4):
        if time.time() > dl - 4:
            break
        p = h_to_p(best_h, best_n) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, max(1, best_n - 1))
        p = np.clip(p, -40.0, 40.0)
        h_try = project_feasible(_h_from_p(p, best_n), best_n)
        hh, ch = polish_lse(h_try, best_n, (2000, 20000, 100000), dl - 2)
        if ch < best_c:
            best_c, best_h = ch, hh

    if best_n % 2 == 0 and 4 <= best_n <= 1000 and time.time() < dl - 1:
        hb, cb = local_search_binary(best_h, best_n, dl - 1, rng)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c, best_h = cb, hb

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
